# Excalidraw Data

## Text Elements
HARMONICS are types of OVERTONES 
that are whole number multiples of the 
pure tone/fundemental frequency ^IruEodn0

OVERTONES and HARMONICS can vary 
in amplitude/loudness, which gives each 
sound its distinct timbre ^GMWmpLge

*not to scale ^JTeJ4li6

SOUND, like other waves, reflect off surfaces ^9sXOoEPW

- HARD surfaces reflect light better than
- SOFT surfaces, which absorb a portion of the sound wave's energy ^w8NGPPrR

- heard seperately from the original
- requires at least 0.1s delay to be distinguished
- at 343m/s this means sound must travel 34.3m ^6YPexypN

- no clear delay, original and reflected sounds merge into one prolonged sound
- happens when reflection delay is < 0.1s
- can last longer than the original sound ^H6XqNAl9

SOUND can bend around obstacles ^zbD86zxD

- HOT air makes sound lose more energy than
- COLD air, which it travels faster in ^XlWLsppi

SOUND can change speeds in 
different materials and temperatures ^QfVZv5Ts

*this is redrawn from memory lolk
 ^jfEcz73i
